### 9.1 Track C — unique monsters uncolored / minion skeletons gold — **uniques FIELD-VERIFIED R49 CLOSED (issue #9); skeleton symptom ROOT-CAUGHT v2 + FIXED R51, field-verify pending (issue #8)**
- **Field-verified (owner, 2026-10-31)**: rebuilt and run — owner verbatim: *"R49 Field Tested, Confirmed Unique Monsters are Unique again."*; documented publicly in **[issue #9](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/9)** ("[Video] Unique Mobs don't have Unique Colors") via the closing-comment writeup (`ISSUE-9-CLOSE-COMMENT.md`). What closed: manifest symptom #2 (unique monsters base colors). The skeleton symptom (#3, issue #8) stayed open → R50 above.
- **R49 root cause (this round)**: `PALSHIFT_GetMonsterTransform` (`D2Client/GAME/PalShift.cpp:295`) early-returned null on a missing `palshift.dat` entry BEFORE the switch — but the rolled unique/champion tint indices 9-29 resolve only through the GLOBAL `RandTransforms.dat` table (30 × 0x100, `gbPalShiftRandTransformLoaded`), which needs no entry. Every random unique tinted → looked up through a dead path → base palette. Fix: entry-out deleted; identical guards moved into the case 0/1 and case 2-7 arms. Gate `palshift: rand-transform reachable (R49)`, suite 203/203. Full record: Rev 53.
- **Owner's proposal (issue #9 comment, 2026-10-30) evaluated and RETIRED**: its target mechanism (MonStats2 components → unit data) is intact end-to-end (loader `HDv`..`S8v` linkers → `MONSTER_RollComponentAppearance` in `MONSTER_InitStats` → 0xac wire with per-component widths derived from `aComponents` → client copy → composite). The wire-width math proves `aComponents[i]` is a variant COUNT, so the proposal's memcpy would write counts into index fields (out-of-range variants) — the correct mechanism already rolls. The proposal's SYMPTOM reading ("components never make it to the client") pointed at the right observable; the actual site was the tint lookup, one layer up. Provenance: all seven chain functions identical `44e3518` → main (`scripts/rev49_trackc_provenance.sh`).
- **Not a dropped fix** (verified rev42): main-vs-720p content-only diffs of `PalShift.cpp` / `Pallete.cpp` / `MonUnique.cpp` are decompiler-noise (guard inversions, `n > 0` ↔ `0 < n`, include order); no color fix exists anywhere in the H21 ledger. This is first-time work on upstream behavior — and R49 confirmed the defect is a BASELINE transcription miss, not a merge casualty.
- **Mechanism map (where retail colors live)**:
  - Static per-class palettes: `D2Client/UNIT/Monster.cpp:1238–1298` — default `TransLvl+2` (clamped <8), Andariel/Duriel (`0x9c`/`0xd3`) take difficulty palettes 3/4, `0x14d` → 5; structurally sane on main, **not** the suspect.
  - Random unique transforms (the gold/silver/red rolls): `PALSHIFT_GetMonsterTransform` (`D2Client/GAME/PalShift.cpp:295`) — the `case 0/1: nClassId - 0x16bU` early-out and the `nPalShiftIndex - 8U` bound over the 30 global `RandTransforms.dat` entries (0x1e00 bytes, loaded once); **R49 fixed the entry-out that blocked this path** (see above); `PALSHIFT_LoadMonsterPalShift` (:209) loads per-monster `Data\Global\Monsters\<code>\Cof\palshift.dat` blobs (only special classes ship one); `PALSHIFT_InitPalShiftTable` (:275).
  - Draw-time application: the unit world-draw (`D2Client/Engine/Gfx.cpp:2925`) fetches `PALSHIFT_GetMonsterTransform(pUnit)` (when the unit has no act palette) and passes the 256-byte remap sub-table to `D2GFX_DrawImage(..., pPalette)` (:2996) per component layer — `GFXUTIL_GetItemColorForBodyLoc` passes monsters through unchanged (players only); `CUnit::SetPalShiftIndex`/getter (`D2Client/UNIT/CUnit.cpp:164-182`) store the index on `pGfxInfo->nPalShiftIndex`.
  - Unique seed → transform roll: `MonUnique.cpp` (`sNewSeed.nSeedLow % 100`); master→minion inheritance is the shared mechanism behind symptoms #2 and #3 — if the master's roll is lost but minions read a default, you get exactly "uniques plain, minions gold".
- **Pattern to hunt first (defect-12 precedent)**: a Ghidra transcription artifact pinning the transform index to one constant — "everything gold" is what a pinned index looks like. Audit the index math as constants/int-casts before touching logic.
- **Cheap first data ask**: one 800x600 D3D screenshot of a unique pack + whether ALL uniques are affected or act/class-specific; note whether champion (blue/orange) naming still works (same pack-generation path).
- **When the track opens**: sweep the PR branches (`jaenster-main`, `pr2`) for color-related fixes before writing anything; every fix lands with a gate row (§5 rule).

## H27. Track I — the 720p belt hover popup arc (issue #14) — ROOT-CAUGHT + FIXED R52, FIELD-VERIFIED R52, CLOSED (2026-10-31)

*One defect, the complete arc, offloaded from handoff.md at rev 59 when the owner field-confirmed the fix. Opened at rev 57 (owner report on the R51 smoke pass: "[720p] Hover Belt Slots do not stay open"; the owner filed it publicly as issue #14), root-caught and fixed the same round (R52: site-6 Y virtualization + tooltip anchor), the gate suite un-pinned around it at rev 58 (owner: gates are for features and fixes), and field-verified + CLOSED at rev 59 on the first field pass — no follow-up defect. The §9.5 record below is verbatim as it stood at rev 58; the closure record follows. Public writeup: `ISSUE-14-CLOSE-COMMENT.md`.*

- **Owner symptom (verbatim)**: "[720p] Hover Belt Slots do not stay open" ([issue #14](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/14), R51 smoke pass, 2026-10-31) — the expanded belt popup opens, then immediately closes on the next mouse event.
- **Root cause (R52)**: `PANEL_CalculateBeltSlotIndex` (`D2Client/UI/Panel.cpp`) — the shared expanded-belt hit-test funnel (~30 call sites: `Inv.cpp` mouse handlers, `SpellSel.cpp`, `ui.cpp`, `D2Client.cpp`) — had the site-5 half only: mouse X virtualized into 800-space (`nVirtualMouseX = nMouseX - VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetX()`), but the Y pair compared RAW against the Belts.txt `nScreenMode=1` rect bounds (600-absolute: the `pad0x01` top read and `beltBoxes[3].nBottom`). At 1280x720 `VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()` = 60 (`ScreenSizeY - 600`), so the real cursor sat one height-delta BELOW the tested rect — every expanded-belt in-rect test failed — and `PANEL_UpdatePanelFromMode` collapsed `gnPanelBeltRowsExpanded` on the next mouse event (Panel.cpp: the `!bFlag` arm sets `gnPanelBeltRowsExpanded = 0`). The OPEN path — the small HUD rect (`ScreenSizeY - 0x27..-10` band), real screen coords — is resolution-independent and worked, hence **opens-then-instantly-closes**. The click resolver `PANEL_GetPanelLayoutHeight` already carries BOTH halves (its site-6 comment names this exact failure mode: "the hit test must follow the bottom-anchored bar art too, or clicks land one height-delta above the drawn slots") — the hover test never got the Y half. **Same-class secondary defect (fixed same round)**: the belt tooltip anchor — `gnBeltTooltipX` rides site-5, but `gnBeltTooltipY = aInvRect[2]` was raw 600-space while the consumer `TEXT_TooltipSetAttributes` takes real screen coords (cf. the DrawGoldAmount call at Panel.cpp) — the item-name tooltip would draw 60px high at 720p once the popup stayed open (owner never saw it: the popup did not stay open long enough).
- **Not a merge casualty — an un-migrated fix site**: the site-5/site-6 sweep covered draw sites and the click resolver but missed the hover-test funnel; same "half-applied resolution" family as track F, caught by field test instead of an audit (the audit scripts check placement/branch of VP-marked sites — this site had the X marker and looked done).
- **Fix (R52)**: `nVirtualMouseY = nMouseY - VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()` feeding both Y bounds of the expanded-rect test; tooltip anchor `gnBeltTooltipY = aInvRect[2] + VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()` with the site-6 marker. Retail no-op: the Y offset is `max(0, ScreenSizeY-600)` = 0 at 640x480/800x600.
- **Gates (R52)**: `site-6: belt hover Y virtualization (R52)`, `site-6: belt tooltip Y anchor (R52)`, Y-shift count re-pinned 17→19. Audits re-run at R52: placement (OK=48/MOVED=139/NEW=153/GONE=2, unchanged), wrong-branch (61 checked, 0 hits — +1 = the new VP-marked tooltip anchor store). Suite at R52 was **208/208 including the banner + build-version pins**; Rev 58 removed the version pins (owner: the gate is for features and fixes) → **206/206**; build self-ID is a manual check (d2boot.log line 1, SMOKE-CHECKLIST item 0).
- **Field-verify ask**: rebuild (R52 — source round; first d2boot.log mark must read `Vanilla+0.0.6-R52`); 720p — hover the belt: popup opens AND stays open while the cursor is inside; slot clicks use/drop the potion (same funnel); filled-slot tooltip anchored to the slot, not 60px high; 640x480 + 800x600 belt hover unchanged (offsets 0 there); R51 skeleton asks (issue #8) still pending.

**Closure (rev 59, 2026-10-31)**: owner on the R52 build: **"R52, Hover belt confirmed fix"** — the popup opens AND stays open while the cursor is inside, slot clicks use/drop the potion, tooltip anchored to the slot. Every §9.5 field-verify ask passed except the R51 skeleton item (issue #8), which belongs to track C and stays open there. Track I CLOSED; `ISSUE-14-CLOSE-COMMENT.md` is the paste-ready issue close comment.

## H28. Track J — the A1Q1 Den-of-Evil turn-in arc (issue #13) — ROOT-CAUGHT + FIXED R54, FIELD-VERIFIED R54, CLOSED (2026-10-31)

- **Issue**: owner, 2026-10-31 ([issue #13](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/13)): "A1Q1 Den of Evil does not complete after killing all monsters in Den of Evil"; field report: a character that killed all Den monsters in a previous game — quest panel says "Return to Akara for a reward", Akara has the `!` — but the turn-in talk gives nothing. Track J opened at Rev 61; the paste-ready body shipped as `ISSUE-13-BODY.md`.
- **R53 instrumentation round** (self-ID → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R53`; suite 206 → 218/218): 13 `quest:` marks along the whole turn-in loop — activate record dump + gossip-line pick, rejoin restore ×6 outcomes, per-kill counter, `den CLEARED`, state-5 broadcast, gossip dispatch, turn-in gate REJECTED/GRANTED, 0x5D sync, client `c2s 0x31 gossip send`. Provenance audit: ALL A1Q1.cpp hunks vs `44e3518` are provably-equivalent churn — the turn-in gate, Callback13 and the kill handler carry NO logic delta.
- **R53 field log decoded — the failing site caught in one run**: rejoin took `FinishedInPreviousGame` (vanilla-shape branch); activate logged `rewardAwaits=1 completed=0 ...` (**T1 ELIMINATED — the server record is correct at talk time**) and `gossip line 3` (server picked the reward line); then **nothing** — no `c2s 0x31`, no gate/grant marks, no in-game c2s traffic until leave (**T2 CONFIRMED — the client never echoed the turn-in id**). T3 (dispatch routing) never came into play.
- **Root cause (R54, static)**: `AddGossipMessageOnMonster` reads `pQuestData->pNpcMessages[slot]` — an array of packed 0xC4 `D2NPCMessageTableStrc` slots — but the regen emitted `A1Q1::aNpcGossipData` **SINGULAR** (1 slot = only the accept entry `{0x94, 0x40, menu 0}`), identical at `44e3518` → a **baseline regen truncation**, the DATA twin of the R49–R51 code-defect class (the quest module carries zero fork edits). `gossip line 3` read +0x24C past the object; no `nStringIndex = 0x4c` entry exists anywhere in the tree data (repo-wide grep: zero hits) → the s2c 0x27 batch carried no quest scroll msg → client `OVERHEAD_GetMessageType0(ptr, 0)` = 0xffff → `UI_HandleNpcInteractionPacket` fell to a no-echo menu branch → **c2s 0x31 never sent** → the correct server gate was never asked. Quest accept (slot 0) was in-bounds — why accept+clear always worked while the reward step died.
- **Fix (R54; self-ID → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R54`)**: `globals/D2Game.cpp` — `A1Q1::aNpcGossipData` and `A2Q1::aNpcGossipData` (Radament/Atma, same family shape) → **5-slot arrays**: slot 0 = accept preserved; slot 3 = turn-in entry `{0x94, 0x4c, menu 0}` / `{0xb0, 0x14e, menu 0}` — ids **pinned by each quest's own turn-in gate** (A1Q1 `Q01_Callback11` 0x40/0x4c; A2Q1 gate 0x130/0x14e); flavor slots 1/2/4 left EMPTY (string ids not in the tree — not invented). **Build-repair rider**: the owner's Win11 llvm-mingw build caught the `extern` declarations in `globals/globals.h` (A1Q1 :4859, A2Q1 :4913) still singular (`conflicting declaration`) — re-declared `[5]` with self-identifying comments, and the two `pNpcMessages = &X::aNpcGossipData` inits became plain array-decay assignments (the A1Q4/A2Q2/A5Q2 convention). **13 new chain marks** (VP `[quest-gossip-mark]`/`[gossip-table-fix]`): server `gossip add` outcomes (EMPTY/NO CLASS MATCH/added) + `npc interact 0x27 send`; client `s2c 0x27 overhead` + `msgType0=` + six `npc menu:` no-echo branches. Gates: 11 new rows (7 mark/data + 4 build-repair pins; the R53 echo-mark row re-pinned 2→8), suite **218 → 229/229**. Sandbox g++ harness verified the packed 0xC4 aggregate layout and slot values. Family audit: ~18 further singular-table quests with slot ≥ 1 call sites (A1Q0, A1Q2, A1Q6, A1Q7, A2Q0, A2Q7, A3Q0, A3Q5, A3Q6, A4Q1, A4Q3, A4Q4, A5Q1, Quest37/38/39/40) — flavor/reward lines absent; recovery needs vanilla data (owner's call) — watch moved to handoff §6.
- **Field verification (Rev 64, 2026-10-31)**: owner on the R54 build, log pasted in-session: **"Great Job. Works."** — the full 7-step chain in one run, first try: `quest: rejoin Den FinishedInPreviousGame (no restore)` → `quest: akara activate classId=0x0094 rewardAwaits=1 completed=0 ...` → `gossip line 3` → `quest: gossip add quest=0x01 slot=3 npc=0x0094` + `str=0x004c menu=0 -> added` → `quest: npc interact 0x27 send npc=0x0094 type=1 msgs=1` → `s2c 0x27 overhead msgType0=0x2cb004c` (raw dword print; the menu-decoded value is `0x004c`) → `npc menu: msgType0=0x004c` → `c2s 0x31 gossip send npc=0000000c gossip=0x004c` → `quest: akara gossip msg=0x004c` → **`quest: den reward GRANTED (+1 skill, Den->Completed, Respec armed)`**. Post-grant activate reads `rewardAwaits=0 completed=1`; participant line 4 → slot 4 EMPTY → generic menu (the designed fallback — flavor slots intentionally not invented). The build-repair rider is field-proven too (the extern fix compiled clean). **Watch hit (same log)**: two `roster heal: ... 64-node cap (corrupt chain, degraded)` marks during load, right after the 0x5B join (SP, party=-1) — the §6 roster-corruptor watch fired for the first time on the 0.0.6-era build; contained by the rev34–36 immunization (session normal, no symptom); §6 updated with the two walk names for the next narrowing pass.
- **Closure**: track J CLOSED on the first field pass of the fix. `ISSUE-13-CLOSE-COMMENT.md` is the paste-ready issue close comment. Remaining open work after closure: **track C skeleton symptom (issue #8) only**, plus the §6 watches (roster-heal hit, ~18-quest gossip-data recovery).

### Rev journal, revs 57–70 (verbatim)

*Rev 70 (2026-10-31, docs round — **track K FIELD-PROVEN through R59 + consolidated release notes R52→R59**, zero source changes, self-ID stays `Vanilla+0.0.6-R59`): owner verdict on the R59 build: **"Great job. iLvl spacing appears fixed."** — the double-gap before "(N)" is gone in the field; the item-ilvl arc (R55 feature → R56 name-suffix → R57 bare digits → R59 single-space trim) is field-proven end to end. Track K stays in the OPEN list pending the owner's closure call (the track I-style archive ritual — §2/§7/§9 rows + HISTORY move — is a separate round on request). Gates re-run sandbox-side: **240/240** (docs-only round — no Application.cpp bump, no gate re-pin; §8). Overlay: `vanilla-plus-0.0.6-R59-release-notes-r52-r59.zip` (release notes + handoff).*)

*Rev 69 (2026-10-31, track K spacing polish — **the gap before "(N)" is now always exactly one space — CODED R59**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R58` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R59`**): owner's one-message round — issue #15 verdict first: **"Appears fixed in R58"** (track L field-proven; R58 stands as shipped), then the last cosmetic ask: *"Sometimes there is more than one space between Item Name and (#)".* Root cause (upstream of us, in-tree): the tooltip name line is composed by `GetItemName` (Inv.cpp:6888) by gluing string-table entries — base name (after `INV_SkipColorCodeSuffix`'s `[..]` token strip), magic affix words (quality-4 branch wsprintfs prefix + base + suffix straight from the magicaffix locale strings), unique/set/rare names, the 0xf9e separator — and several of those entries end with their own trailing space, so the R57 bare-digit append landed after one and rendered `Name  (12)`; data-driven, hence "sometimes" (worst case: magic items whose suffix affix word is the last component). **Fix (fork-owned TU only, one fn — zero decomp edits)**: `ITEMILVL_AppendNameSuffix` (VanillaPlusCore.cpp) now strips the caller name's trailing spaces/tabs before appending (hand-rolled wide scan, no new imports); the gap before (N) is always exactly the one space the format owns. The trim sits below every early-out, so OFF still never writes the buffer (charter rule 1); trimming only shrinks the name, so headroom vs the ≤ 7-wchar suffix only gets wider. The three Inv.cpp call sites, data source, 2 s hot-read gate, Options row, ini template: untouched. **Gates**: +1 row "ilvl: trailing-space trim (R59)" pinning the trim loop (`nNameLen--`), ilvl block 9 → 10 rows, suite **239 → 240/240**. No d2boot marks beyond the self-ID bump — display-only, the visible spacing IS the mark (R55/R56/R57 precedent). **Docs**: FEATURES §3 + SMOKE §C 12b carry the one-space check. **Field-verify ask (one hover, §C 12b)**: with `item_ilvl=1`, hover a magic item with a suffix affix (the old worst case) + a unique: gap before `(N)` is exactly one space on every item; OFF → pixel-identical.*)

*Rev 68 (2026-10-31, track L field-fix round — **issue #15 root-caught: the Rev-23 stale-selection fix was DEAD CODE — FIXED R58**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R57` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R58`**): owner filed **[issue #15](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/15)** ("Standing within pickup radius of an item and clicking away to move picks up item") while R57 built. **This is the Rev-23 / ledger-row-37 defect REOPENED — that fix never worked** (row 37 still `pending user land`, no field round ever confirmed it). **Root cause (all legs verified in-tree)**: (1) toggle mode's two stale-selection unload clauses (ShowItems.cpp mid-loop sprite-spare + tail clear, both added Rev 23, patches 0016+0017) call `UNIT::CUnit::HitTestScreen(pItem, x, y, nullptr)` — and HitTestScreen (CUnit.cpp:2352) short-circuits a null 4th arg at `if (!pObjectsTxt) return 1;` (:2446) = validity-only, NO geometry, point ignored; (2) so the sprite-hit test is always-true → the mid-loop revoke is unreachable in toggle mode (the spare clause always fires) and the tail unload's `!HitTest(...)` is always-false → both dead; (3) the label-hover pin (`SELECT_UpdateUnitSelection(item)` → `bCanSelectUnit=1`) therefore never clears while labels are latched: the per-frame recompute no-ops at its lock check (Select.cpp:779), `GetSelectedUnit` re-validates through the same nullptr short-circuit (on-screen-only semantics — Rev-23 was right that a pinned item never self-clears), and the Alt-keyup wipe (`CMD_ReleaseHoldAltAndClearSelection`) only fires on Alt transitions; (4) vanilla `ClickMap` (Player.cpp:2077-2083) reads the selected unit and REWRITES the click coords to it for UNIT_ITEM/UNIT_OBJECT targets — net: one label brush pins the item selection forever, EVERY world click retargets to it, within pickup radius = instant pickup on click-away (issue #15 verbatim), farther = walk-to-item. Vanilla hold-Alt unaffected (the added clauses are dead there by design; hold mode byte-for-byte vanilla). **Fix (ShowItems.cpp only, 2 args)**: both sprite-hit tests pass `(D2ObjectsTxt*)0x1` — Select.cpp's own real-geometry idiom for item candidates (:796/:855); on the ITEM path the pointer is never dereferenced (run-mode + hold-alt checks, then unit-frame sprite bounds), so the test becomes the REAL point-in-sprite-bounds at the cursor. Semantics restored as designed: label click picks up, sprite click picks up (spare now truthful), click on empty ground walks (stale pin cleared the next draw frame), combat clicks attack (non-item selections were never pinned); hold mode unchanged. **Known residual (vanilla-parity)**: a same-frame flick+click can dispatch before the next draw's unload — the same one-frame window vanilla has for every unit type; the forever-pin is gone. **Docs**: `ISSUE-15-ROOT-CAUSE.md` (paste-ready issue comment, ISSUE-14 format); SMOKE-CHECKLIST §C item 8 carries the R58 regression guard. **Gates**: NEW row `alt-toggle: stale-clear real sprite hit` pinning the real-geometry marker x2 in ShowItems.cpp (comment text worded around the literal so only the two call sites count), suite **238 → 239/239**. **Self-ID**: Application.cpp:66 → R58 (R57 ships as-built with the ilvl bare-digit format; R58 = this fix on top; the R58 overlay carries BOTH rounds' files so it applies cleanly over R56 or R57). No d2boot marks by design — behavioral fix, SMOKE §C item 8 IS the mark; roster-heal + ~18-quest watches unchanged. **Field-verify asks (one run, SMOKE §C item 8 R58 guard)**: labels latched → brush a label, walk-click empty ground = WALK (no pickup, no walk-to-loot); label click = pickup; sprite click = pickup; combat click = attack; alt_toggle=0 unchanged.*)

*Rev 67 (2026-10-31, track K presentation polish — **suffix drops the "ilvl" label: name reads "Long Sword (12)" — CODED R57**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R56` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R57`**): owner field verdict on R56 first — **"Good job, that's better"** (the name-suffix presentation is field-proven) — then the ask: *"Instead of saying (ilvl #) we can just say (#) they know it's the ilvl."* Shipped: the one string that changed is `ITEMILVL_AppendNameSuffix`'s SStrPrintf format, `" (ilvl %i)"` → `" (%i)"` — with `[features] item_ilvl=1` the tooltip name line now reads `Long Sword (12)`; data source (`ITEM_GetItemData_nItemLevel`, offset 0x2C), the `ITEMILVL_Active()` 2 s hot-read gate, the three Inv.cpp call sites (LoadItemDesc identified + unidentified, ITEM_BuildSetItemTooltip) and the OFF byte-identical semantics are all untouched — buffer headroom only gets wider (bare digits ≤ 7 wchars vs the old ≤ 12). **d2vp.ini template** comment updated to the bare-digit shape (`1 = append " (N)"`, example `Long Sword (12)`; existing files keep working — lazy key read). **Gates**: suffix-format pin re-targeted `" \(ilvl %i\)"` → `" \(%i\)"`, row count unchanged (9 rows), suite **238 → 238/238**. *Rev 66 (2026-10-31, track K presentation round — **item ilvl moves INTO the name line — CODED R56**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R55` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R56`**): owner field verdict on the R55 build first — **"Great job. iLvl works."** (the standalone-line feature is field-proven) — then the ask: *"Instead of adding a whole Item level line to the tooltip, can we append the item name with (ilvl) in the tooltip instead"*. Shipped: with `[features] item_ilvl=1` the tooltip's **name line** reads `Long Sword (ilvl 12)` — no separate `Item Level:` row, no extra tooltip height; OFF = the suffix fn returns without touching the name buffer, so the decomp builders stay byte-identical to retail. **Mechanics (data source unchanged from R55)**: `D2Common::ITEMS::Items::ITEM_GetItemData_nItemLevel` (item-data offset 0x2C); suffix is hardcoded ASCII `" (ilvl %i)"` via SStrPrintf (no MPQ edits; fork rule). **Wiring**: `VanillaPlusCore.{h,cpp}` — `ITEMILVL_FormatLine` (line writer) replaced by **`ITEMILVL_AppendNameSuffix(pItem, wszName)`** — same `ITEMILVL_Active()` 2 s hot-read gate (cache + D2VP_ConfigReloaded bust unchanged); the suffix joins the `GetItemName` buffer BEFORE the caller's `AppendAsWideChar` color wrap, so it renders in the item's own name color (gold on uniques, green on complete set pieces, grey-white on normals); early-outs: gate off / null-or-empty name / ilvl < 1. **Inv.cpp** — the R55 line plumbing is fully REMOVED (both builders' `wszItemLevel[64]` locals, clear loops, format calls, `AppendAsWideChar(wszItemLevel, 0)` appends — 8 sites) and the suffix call lands at the **three** `GetItemName` sites: `LoadItemDesc` identified path, `LoadItemDesc` unidentified path (suffix still shows on unid items — ilvl is client-known), `ITEM_BuildSetItemTooltip` (set pieces); all `// VP: [item-ilvl] name suffix` marked. Coverage unchanged: shop-narrow (name suffix flows through `INV_FormatItemTooltipWithCost` like the line did), book / transmogrify tooltips stay vanilla by design (v1 scope). **Tooltip-order note (why the name edit is safe)**: the D2 tooltip string is assembled bottom-up and drawn upward (`D2WINFONT_DrawWideString` newline advances `nY` by `-lineHeight*0.9`) — the name is appended LAST in `wszWorkBuf` yet renders FIRST; editing the name buffer only affects the one line it already owns. **Options page**: row 5 `Item Level Tooltips` label and key unchanged (same feature, new presentation; no row/geometry edits). **d2vp.ini template**: `item_ilvl` block comment rewritten to the suffix shape (existing files keep working — lazy key read). **Self-ID**: Application.cpp:66 → R56. **Gates**: ilvl block re-pinned 9 → 9 rows (formatter row → suffix fn, call-sites row 2 → 3 sites, buffer-sites row dropped, NEW suffix-format-string pin `" \(ilvl %i\)"` 1), suite **238 → 238/238**. No d2boot marks by design — display-only, the visible name suffix IS the mark (R55 precedent); roster-heal + ~18-quest watches unchanged. **Field-verify asks (one run, §C 12b)**: d2vp.ini `item_ilvl=1` → hover items: name reads `<Name> (ilvl N)` on the name line, NOTHING below Required Level changed; sanity pairs — early-act normal (ilvl 1-3ish) vs late-game unique (gold suffix, higher N), a set piece (set builder path; suffix green when the set is complete), an unidentified item (suffix still shows); OFF → tooltips pixel-identical; options row flips + hot-applies ≤ 2 s.*

*Rev 65 (2026-10-31, track K source round — **NEW FEATURE: item iLvl tooltips — CODED R55**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R54` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R55`**): owner: "Ok let's add a new Vanilla+ Feature, Item iLvl". Shipped **`item_ilvl`** — the D2R "Advanced Tooltips" parity (ilvl readout half): with `[features] item_ilvl=1` in d2vp.ini, every item tooltip gains one line — `Item Level: <ilvl>` — anchored directly below the retail "Required Level" line (or in its slot when the item has none); default/missing/garbage = 0 = vanilla byte-for-byte. **Mechanics**: the ilvl data source is the client's own item unit — `D2Common::ITEMS::Items::ITEM_GetItemData_nItemLevel` (item-data offset 0x2C, the same parsed-item struct the retail req-level line already reads client-side; ilvl travels in the item bitstream on every path that reaches the tooltip — dropped, bought, gambled, traded, socketed, mercenary). **Wiring** (charter shape: fork-owned TU + VP-marked call sites): `VanillaPlusCore.{h,cpp}` — `ITEMILVL_Active()` (the shared 2 s hot-read cache, globe_persistent shape — the tooltip rebuilds every frame the cursor dwells, so no rebuild trigger is needed) + `ITEMILVL_FormatLine(pItem, wszText)` (writes the line or an EMPTY wide string; OFF = the unconditionally-appended upstream buffers are no-ops — `AppendToBuffer` copies just the terminator, `AppendAsWideChar` returns empty buffers untouched — so the decomp builders stay byte-identical to retail); line idiom = INV_FormatReqStrText's (label, white code locale 0xf9b, `%i` digits via SStrPrintf, line separator locale 0xf9e; label hardcoded ASCII — the vanilla string table has no "Item Level" entry and the fork never edits MPQ data). `Inv.cpp` — two call sites: `LoadItemDesc` (all normal items: identified + unidentified paths, uniques, rares, whites, mercenary items via the :11484 caller) and `ITEM_BuildSetItemTooltip` (set pieces); each = `wszItemLevel[64]` local + clear loop + format call + `AppendAsWideChar(wszItemLevel, 0)` append in the assembly, all `// VP: [item-ilvl]` marked. Shop-narrow / book / transmogrify tooltips stay vanilla by design (v1 scope). **Options page**: row 5 `{ "item_ilvl", L"Item Level Tooltips" }`, `VPP_ROW_COUNT/BACK` 4 → 5 (geometry safe: row idx 4 top 0xD0+0x1a = 0xEA, back row 0x124); hot-reload via D2VP_ConfigReloaded (cache bust added). **d2vp.ini template**: documented `item_ilvl=0` block (existing files keep working — the gate reads the key lazily, the template only matters for fresh creates). **Self-ID**: Application.cpp:66 → R55. **Gates**: 9 new rows (core gate+formatter+key+template+cache-bust, header decls, Inv.cpp call sites + buffer sites, options row), suite **229 → 238/238**. No d2boot marks by design — display-only feature, the visible tooltip line IS the mark (globe_persistent precedent); roster-heal + ~18-quest watches unchanged. **Field-verify asks (one run, §C 12b)**: d2vp.ini `item_ilvl=1` → hover items: line reads `Item Level: <ilvl>` under Required Level; sanity pairs — early-act normal item (ilvl 1-3ish), a late-nm/hell unique (higher), a set piece (set builder path), an unidentified item (line still shows); OFF → tooltips pixel-identical; options page row flips + hot-applies ≤ 2 s.*

*Rev 63 (2026-10-31, track J source round — **A1Q1 Den turn-in ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED R54 — the Den reward gossip line never existed in the tree**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R53` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R54`**): owner field pass on the R53 build (log pasted in-session) — the instrumentation caught the failing site in one run: `quest: rejoin Den FinishedInPreviousGame (no restore)` (vanilla-shape branch, byte-identical region), `quest: akara activate classId=0x0094 rewardAwaits=1 completed=0 justCompleted=0 stateFlag=1 introduced=0` (**T1 ELIMINATED — the server record is correct at talk time**), `quest: akara activate -> gossip line 3` (server picked the reward line), then **nothing** — no `c2s 0x31 gossip send`, no `quest: akara gossip msg=`, no gate/grant marks, no in-game c2s traffic until leave (**T2 CONFIRMED**: the client never echoed the turn-in id). Root cause (static, proven on the data): `AddGossipMessageOnMonster` reads `pQuestData->pNpcMessages[slot]` — an array of `D2NPCMessageTableStrc` (packed 0xC4: 16 `(npcClassId, stringId, menu)` entries + count) — but the regen emitted `A1Q1::aNpcGossipData` **SINGULAR** (1 slot = only the accept entry `{0x94, 0x40, menu 0}`), identical at `44e3518` → a **baseline regen truncation** (the data twin of the R49–R51 code-defect class; quest module carries zero fork edits). `gossip line 3` read +0x24C past the object; no `nStringIndex = 0x4c` entry exists anywhere in the tree data (repo-wide grep zero hits) → the 0x27 batch carried no quest scroll msg → client `OVERHEAD_GetMessageType0(ptr, 0)` = 0xffff → `UI_HandleNpcInteractionPacket` fell to the interaction/generic no-echo path → **c2s 0x31 never sent** → the (correct) server gate was never asked. Quest accept (slot 0) was in-bounds — why accept+clear always worked. **Fix (R54)**: `globals/D2Game.cpp` — `A1Q1::aNpcGossipData` and `A2Q1::aNpcGossipData` (identical family shape) → 5-slot arrays `[gossip-table-fix R54]`: slot 0 = accept as before, slot 3 = turn-in entry `{0x94, 0x4c, menu 0}` / `{0xb0, 0x14e, menu 0}` — ids **pinned by each quest's own turn-in gate** (A1Q1 `Q01_Callback11` 0x40/0x4c; A2Q1 gate 0x130/0x14e), bMenu 0 mirrors the accept entry (menu 1 would convert to 0 at add time — immaterial); slots 1/2/4 (vanilla flavor lines) left EMPTY — string ids not in the tree, not invented, `count=0` early-out visible in the marks. **Family audit (this round)**: ~18 further quests have singular tables while their handlers use slot ≥ 1 (A1Q0, A1Q2, A1Q6, A1Q7, A2Q0, A2Q7, A3Q0, A3Q5, A3Q6, A4Q1, A4Q3, A4Q4, A5Q1, Quest37/38/39/40) — flavor/reward lines likewise absent/OOB; the 8 array tables (A1Q4[11], A2Q2[11], A2Q4[12], A3Q2[13], A3Q4[9], A5Q2[12], A5Q3[10], A5Q5[9]) are self-contained; repair beyond A1Q1/A2Q1 needs vanilla-data recovery (string ids not derivable in-tree) — follow-up watch below. **Instrumentation R54 (13 new d2boot.log sites, all VP `[quest-gossip-mark]`/`[gossip-table-fix]`**: `QUESTS.cpp` `AddGossipMessageOnMonster` — `quest: gossip add quest= slot= npc=` entry + `EMPTY (count=)` early-out + per-entry `str= menu= -> added` + `NO CLASS MATCH`; `MonsterAI.cpp` `NPC_BeginInteraction` — `quest: npc interact 0x27 send npc= type= msgs=` (the gossip batch leaving); `Text.cpp` `TEXT_HandleOverheadTextPacket` — `s2c 0x27 overhead type= guid= msgs=` + `msgType0=0x%04x (0xffff = no scroll msg...)`; `ui.cpp` `UI_HandleNpcInteractionPacket` — six branch marks: `type 6 quest-record-only`, `initialized shortcut`, `hand-item bail`, `msgType0=0x%04x`, `interaction path`, `generic menu` (each no-echo exit named). **Build-repair rider (owner's Win11 build caught the extern mismatch — same round, still R54)**: the two table DEFINITIONS became `[5]` arrays but the matching `extern` declarations in `globals/globals.h` (A1Q1 :4859, A2Q1 :4913) still said singular → `conflicting declaration` compile errors; fixed the externs to `[5]` with self-identifying `// [R54] issue #13 A1Q1/A2Q1: 5-slot gossip table` comments, and the two init assignments `pNpcMessages = &X::aNpcGossipData` dropped the `&` (an array's `&` is `(*)[5]` — plain decay matches the existing A1Q4/A2Q2/A5Q2 array convention, same address, same semantics). Sandbox g++ harness verified: packed 0xC4 layout, aggregate syntax, slot values (slot3 = 0x94/0x4c/count 1, flavor slots count 0). **Gates**: 11 new rows (7 mark/data rows + 4 build-repair pins: externs ×2, decay assignments ×2; the R53 `client: gossip send VP block` row re-pinned 2→8), suite **218 → 229/229**. `SMOKE-CHECKLIST.md` §F items 15/16 rewritten to the R54 expected-log chain (7 steps ending `quest: den reward GRANTED`). `ISSUE-13-BODY.md` rewritten to the root-caused shape: R53 log decode table, root cause, fix, the 7-step PASS log, family note. Candidates: **T1** server-side state restore, **T2** client never sends 0x4c, **T3** dispatch routing. Log decoded: `op 0x6d` ×10 = D2GS_PING keepalives (benign); `op 0x69` = D2GS_LEAVEGAME → `NET_D2GS_SERVER_LeaveServer` (state-cmd 5 "leaving" + 6 "terminated" after `SaveAllPlayers`); "`s2c recv 0x06 during load`" = the state-6 byte arriving after the exiting client reset `ServerLoadingComplete=0` (one-shot boot mark re-armed) — cosmetic, NOT a quest packet; the Akara talk itself was invisible (mode-1 c2s log caps at 4 packets). **Instrumentation R53 (13 `quest:`-prefixed marks)**: `A1Q1.cpp` — Callback00 activate record dump + picked gossip line (+ participant-4 path), Callback08 per-kill counter + `den CLEARED`, TimerNotifyCompletion state-5 broadcast, Callback13 rejoin restore ×6 outcomes (each logs the SERVER-side `rewardAwaits` bit — mark-only read), Callback11 gossip entry + `turn-in gate REJECTED` + `reward GRANTED`; `QUESTS.cpp` — `QUEST_OnNpcGossip` c2s 0x31 recv mark, `QUEST_SendQuestUpdateToPlayer` Den-only 0x5D sync mark; `ui.cpp` — `c2s 0x31 gossip send` mark (VP `[quest-gossip-mark]`, 2 blocks). **Provenance audited this round**: ALL A1Q1.cpp hunks vs `44e3518` = provably-equivalent churn (De Morgan inversions w/ swapped bodies, `short`→`uint16_t`, `true/false`→`'\x01'/'\0'`) — the turn-in gate, Callback13 and the kill handler carry NO logic delta; QUESTS.cpp gossip + rejoin regions clean; `QuestRecord.cpp` = one equivalent bound rewrite (`iStep < 0xc` → `<= 0xb`); `QuestRecord.h` enum retype `int`→`uint32_t` inert. **Gates**: 12 new rows (R53 quest/client marks), suite **206 → 218/218**. Audits: placement + wrong-branch unchanged (both HEAD-scoped — re-run after the owner commits; the new VP block is include-at-top + mark-after-anchor, placement-trivial). `SMOKE-CHECKLIST.md`: R53 build line + new **section F** (15 fresh-clear same-game turn-in, 16 rejoin turn-in repro — one run captures the whole split). `ISSUE-13-BODY.md` expanded: field report verbatim, the rejoin turn-in chain + T1/T2/T3, the annotated log-decode table, an Instrumentation section, audit-progress bullet. *Rev 57 (2026-10-31, track I open + fix round — **720p belt hover popup collapses — ROOT-CAUGHT + FIXED R52**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R51` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R52`**): owner field report on the R51 smoke pass: **"[720p] Hover Belt Slots do not stay open"** — the expanded belt popup opens, then closes on the next mouse event (**[issue #14](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/14)**, filed by the owner at rev 58). **Root cause**: `PANEL_CalculateBeltSlotIndex` (`D2Client/UI/Panel.cpp`) — the shared expanded-belt hit-test funnel (~30 call sites: Inv.cpp mouse handlers, SpellSel.cpp, ui.cpp, D2Client.cpp) — had the site-5 half only: mouse X virtualized into 800-space (`nVirtualMouseX`), but the Y pair compared RAW against the Belts.txt nScreenMode=1 rect's 600-absolute bounds. At 1280x720 `VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()` = 60, so the real cursor sat one height-delta BELOW the tested rect — every expanded-belt in-rect test failed, and `PANEL_UpdatePanelFromMode` collapsed `gnPanelBeltRowsExpanded` on the next mouse event (the OPEN path — the small HUD rect, real screen coords — is resolution-independent and worked, hence opens-then-instantly-closes). The click resolver `PANEL_GetPanelLayoutHeight` already carries BOTH halves (its site-6 comment describes exactly this failure mode) — the hover test never got the Y half. Same-class secondary defect fixed in the same round: the belt tooltip anchor `gnBeltTooltipY = aInvRect[2]` was raw 600-space while `gnBeltTooltipX` rides site-5, and the consumer `TEXT_TooltipSetAttributes` takes real screen coords — the item-name tooltip would draw 60px high once the popup stayed open (owner never saw it — the popup did not stay open long enough). **Fix (R52)**: `nVirtualMouseY = nMouseY - VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()` feeding both Y bounds (offset 0 at 640x480/800x600 — retail byte-identical); tooltip anchor rides the re-centered rect. Gates: `site-6: belt hover Y virtualization (R52)`, `site-6: belt tooltip Y anchor (R52)`, Y-shift count re-pinned 17→19; version gates re-pinned R51→R52 (banner + build version). Suite: **208/208**. Audits re-run: placement (OK=48/MOVED=139/NEW=153/GONE=2, unchanged), wrong-branch (61 checked, 0 hits — +1 = the new VP-marked tooltip anchor store). **Also in this round's source delta (rider, owner directive "let's make the .log file not accumulate, always overwrite")**: `support/d2_boot.cpp` opens d2boot.log with GENERIC_WRITE + CREATE_ALWAYS (was FILE_APPEND_DATA + OPEN_ALWAYS) — fresh log every run, truncate-once at the first mark, hangwatch/crash writes ride the same handle; gate `boot: log overwrites per run`; plus `SMOKE-CHECKLIST.md` (the R51 in-game smoke pass the owner ran, now carrying the 13b belt regression item). **Field-verify asks**: rebuild (R52 — source round), first d2boot.log mark must read `Vanilla+0.0.6-R52`; (1) 720p: hover belt → popup opens AND stays open while the cursor is inside; (2) click slots in the expanded popup → use/drop the potion (clicks share the same funnel); (3) filled-slot tooltip anchored to the slot, not 60px high; (4) 640x480 + 800x600 belt hover unchanged (offsets 0 there); (5) R51 skeleton asks (issue #8) still pending. Overlay: `vanilla-plus-0.0.6-R52-belt-hover-y-virtualization.zip`.


## H30. handoff.md — seventh offload: the pre-R74 round-by-round record (revs 71–91, verbatim)

*Verbatim from handoff.md, moved at rev 94 (2026-11-02) on owner directive ("Update the .md's and trim handoff.md of pre-R74 work"). Covers revs 71–91 — the 2026-11-01 era after H29's block: the track M save-and-re-enter arc to its close (revs 71–73: the R60 static audit + probes, the R61 root-cause — the VP Resolution row's runtime table mutation — + fix, FIELD-VERIFIED "R61 Confirmed fixes #19", issue #19 CLOSED), the 1776–1783 upstream sync chain to its field-run (revs 74–79: merges 3d645e7 + 19f9791 + d6d4b53, the SCmd.cpp include fix, the DrawSolidRectEx Windowed.h link fix, stamped R62, the full SMOKE checklist passed), the docs rounds (revs 80–81: the first handoff trim + the R62 SMOKE record + the track N shared-stash plan), and track N shared-stash round 1 through field-fix and polish (revs 82–91, R63–R70: the 1-page code, the mingw32 LPDWORD build-fix, the two tab-anchor fixes, the 0.0.7 release cut at Rev 86, the Options-page row, the Font24 bump, the gold-display float, the contrast pill + the host syntax-check net — gates 255 → 298) — every round in the block field-proven, closed or superseded. Newest first, exactly as they stood at rev 93; live state stayed in handoff.md (top line + §2/§7/§8); the 1792-merge rounds (revs 92–93, R73/R74) also stayed in handoff.md — the trim boundary.*

### Rev journal, revs 71–91 (verbatim)

*Rev 91 (2026-11-01, compile-fix round — **track N: the R70 pill overlay DID NOT COMPILE — one-line namespace fix, plus a host syntax-check net so text gates are never the last line of defense again — R70 → R71**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.7-R70` → `Vanilla+0.0.7-R71`**, gates **297 → 298**): owner's Win11 build of the R70 overlay died in VanillaPlusCore.cpp 1189 — `error: 'Engine' has not been declared` on the new pill call `Engine::Gfx::D2GFX_DrawSolidRectExBySize(...)`. **Root cause:** `D2Client/Engine/Gfx.h` declares the call inside `namespace D2Client::Engine::Gfx` (Gfx.h 7245), so the SHORT `Engine::Gfx::` form only resolves from TUs sitting inside `namespace D2Client` — `VanillaPlusOptions.cpp` is wrapped in `D2Client::UI::VanillaPlus`, which is why its identical calls (568/588/598) compile; `VanillaPlusCore.cpp` is a global-scope TU whose own line-372 comment documents the rule ("fully qualified: this TU is outside namespace D2Client (D2Client.cpp was inside)"). The R70 round shipped because every sandbox verifier is text-based — the gate suite greps, nothing compiles. **Fix:** the call is now fully qualified as `D2Client::Engine::Gfx::D2GFX_DrawSolidRectExBySize` with a why-comment at the site; the R70 pill gate re-pins the QUALIFIED form and a new must-NOT-match tripwire (`stash: tab pill call qualified (R71)`, `(^|[^:a-zA-Z0-9_])Engine::Gfx::` x0) rejects the bare form in this TU. **The net so this never repeats:** new `tools/syntax-check.sh` (+ `tools/vp_host_shim.h`, `tools/vp_host_api.h`) runs all four fork-owned TUs (`VanillaPlusCore.cpp`, `VanillaPlusOptions.cpp`, `VanillaPlusStash.cpp`, `support/d2_crash.cpp`) through the host g++ with the build's exact flag shape (`-std=c++17 -w -fms-extensions`, d2_platform.h + support/d2_hand.h force-included, CMake's D2_HEADERS-derived include dirs), `-fsyntax-only` — full parse + name lookup, seconds per TU, no objects. d2_platform.h's never-compiled non-Windows path needed three accommodations, all confined to the tool (zero repo files edited): (1) the shim prelude force-included FIRST — glibc FILE-guard reconciliation (`__FILE_defined`/`__io_FILE_defined`), `__stdcall`/`__fastcall`/`__cdecl`/`__thiscall` macros, `errno_t`, and the few Win32 SDK structs the decompiled headers import (real x86 `_FLOATING_SAVE_AREA`; opaque `_devicemodeA`/`tagWNDCLASSA`/`tagCANDIDATELIST`/`_SERVICE_STATUS`/`_SYSTEM_INFO` stand-ins; `RGBQUAD`); (2) `vp_host_api.h` force-included AFTER d2_platform.h — the Win32 functions/constants the fork's own ini/file/crash plumbing calls that the non-Windows path lacks (profile strings, lstring, file extras, module/temp-path, NT_TIB/NtCurrentTeb, real PE image headers, MODULEENTRY32, vectored-handler + version surface, `WINAPI`/`CALLBACK`/`CDECL`, TRUE/FALSE/MAX_PATH/INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE/...), every signature canonical so a collision surfaces as a duplicate-declaration error, never papered over; (3) a TEMP SHADOW copy of d2_platform.h (mktemp -d, first on the include path, same file name so #pragma-once identity holds) with five one-line stubs dropped — the `void*` PRTL_CRITICAL_SECTION_DEBUG that conflicts with Storm/_unnamespaced.h's SDK-faithful form, the layout-wrong IMAGE_DOS/NT_HEADERS/SECTION_HEADER stubs, and the one-member CONTEXT stub + its LPCONTEXT typedef (replaced by the SDK-spelled `tag-_CONTEXT` alias the shim provides and Fog/Debug.h — the tree's own non-Windows fallback block — completes) — plus `<windows.h>`/`<tlhelp32.h>` shadow stubs that route the two TUs including them directly onto that same surface (d2_platform aliases + vp_host_api fill-in + Fog/Debug.h bodies). `check-vanilla-plus.sh` now runs the syntax check before its summary: any TU failure fails the suite; a box with no C++ compiler SKIPS the step so the gates stay runnable bare. Sandbox run: **298/298 gates + 4/4 TUs clean (g++ 14.2)**. Zero behavior change vs R70's design — same pill, same geometry (pads stay one-line constants `VPSHAREDUI_TAB_BACKING_*`), same position/anchor/hit-band/down-swallow. **Field-verify ask unchanged from R70 (SMOKE §H item 21):** the word must read as white-on-dark-pill floating above the gold amount box on ANY terrain (bright sand included); green `> Shared` keeps the pill on the shared page; click behavior unchanged (down swallowed, up toggles); 640x480 unchanged. **Docs:** SMOKE build line + item 0 → R71, §H status + item 21 carry the compile-fix note; FEATURES row 9; plan status; README badge 297→298 + status bullet. **Delivery:** overlay `vanilla-plus-0.0.7-R71-tabpillfix.zip` (13 files) + git bundle `vanilla-plus-1776-1783-R71-tabpillfix.bundle` (strict fast-forward, basis origin/main `9b97cea`).*)

*Rev 90 (2026-11-01, field-fix round — **track N: the floating tab word was INVISIBLE — retail text-backdrop pill added under it — R69 → R70**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.7-R69` → `Vanilla+0.0.7-R70`**, gates **294 → 297**): owner field data on the R69 build: "The Stash label is in the right place, clicking around switches the tab. But it is invisible again." — position + the click half CONFIRMED (anchor, hit band and down-swallow all behave), the word itself gone. **Diagnosis (the tab's SECOND invisibility; different mechanism than R85):** not draw-order this time — the late frame queue was swept end to end (Draw.cpp 540-759 plus every called site's rect): automap/overlays draw BEFORE `DRAW_UI_Inventory` (571 / 578-588 < 593), everything after the tab site is bottom- or right-anchored or hover-only (HUD orbs/stamina/menu-button, level-up buttons at `ScreenSizeY−8`, quest-log button, mini-panel at `ScreenSizeY−0x2f`, orb texts at `ScreenSizeY−0x5f`, the party-roster 400x350 backdrop only under the party window, tooltips mouse-anchored) — nothing repaints y 24..50 x 185..300 at 800x600. The word IS drawn every frame (same site, font and colors as the field-proven R66-R68 draws) — what changed is the BACKGROUND: the R66/R68 rows sat on the panel's dark parchment, the R69 float rides the LIVE WORLD strip above the panel, and un-outlined white glyphs wash out on bright terrain — exactly the tradeoff the R69 handoff put on record ("over bright outdoor terrain the un-outlined white text can wash out — say the word and we shadow it"); the R69 comment's "black playfield" only holds over fog/indoors. The owner said the word. **Fix = the retail text-backdrop recipe, nothing moves:** `VPSHAREDUI_DrawTab` now draws a black `DRAW_VERYTRANSPARENT` rect under the word on float rows — the same pattern retail's own `UI_DrawPartyRoster` uses under its roster text (ui.cpp 1506; black + VERYTRANSPARENT = the standard 75% blend per the d2_enums DRAWMODE table) — drawn same-site right before `DRAW_text`, so the word stays the last thing painted on its rect and the glyphs read on any terrain. Geometry: pill = the measured word box + `VPSHAREDUI_TAB_BACKING_PAD_X 4` per side, frame height + `PAD_UP 2` above / `PAD_DOWN 4` below the baseline (Font24 pill ≈ 123×30 for `> Shared`, ~98×30 for `Shared`); follows `[ui] stash_tab_font` live like everything else. Float rows ONLY — `VPSHAREDUI_TabAnchor` grew an optional third out-param (`pbFloating`; the hit band passes 0), and the 640x480 legacy row sits on dark parchment and keeps its field-proven bare look. Position, anchor constants, hit band, down-swallow: UNCHANGED from R69. **Gates**: +3 (`stash: tab backing pill (R70)` pins the `Engine::Gfx::D2GFX_DrawSolidRectExBySize` call, `stash: tab pill translucent (R70)` pins the `0, DRAW_VERYTRANSPARENT` tail, `stash: tab pill float-only (R70)` pins the `if (bFloating) {` branch), self-id re-pinned → suite **297/297**. **Docs**: SMOKE build line + item 0 → R70; §H field status + item 21 carry the R70 verdict + the pill; FEATURES row 9; plan status; README badge 294→297 + status bullet. **Field-verify ask (one eyeball, SMOKE §H item 21)**: the word now reads as white-on-dark-pill floating above the gold amount box on ANY terrain (bright sand included); green `> Shared` keeps the same pill on the shared page; click behavior unchanged (down swallowed, up toggles); 640x480 unchanged. If the pill reads too heavy or too light, the pads are one-line constants (`VPSHAREDUI_TAB_BACKING_PAD_*`). **Delivery**: overlay `vanilla-plus-0.0.7-R70-tabpill.zip` (10 files) + git bundle `vanilla-plus-1776-1783-R70-tabpill.bundle` (strict fast-forward, basis origin/main `9b97cea`).*)

*Rev 89 (2026-11-01, field-polish round — **track N: the "Shared" tab moved — it now FLOATS above the stash panel, over the gold amount display — R68 → R69**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.7-R68` → `Vanilla+0.0.7-R69`**, gates **289 → 294**): owner field data on the R68 build, two messages: "ok its definitely bigger. but now it overlaps with the 'Gold Max' label. Can we move the Stash label to where my cursor is? Up and to the left. *Rev 83 (2026-11-01, fix round — **track N round-1 build-fix: the `.sss` read path vs mingw32's `LPDWORD`, R63 → R64**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R63` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R64`**, gates **280 → 281**): owner's first R63 build failed at exactly one TU — `D2Game/PLAYER/VanillaPlusStash.cpp` (the other 13 overlay files all compiled clean; ninja reached [26/29] with the stash TU the only failure). Root cause: the textbook win32/MinGW32 type identity — on 32-bit Windows `DWORD` is `unsigned long` while `uint32_t` is `unsigned int`, distinct C++ types, so `&dwGot` over a `uint32_t` cannot convert to `ReadFile`'s 4th parameter `LPDWORD` (`long unsigned int*`) and mingw32 correctly rejects it without `-fpermissive`. Fix: the `VPSHARED_TryReadFile` block-read site declares its byte-count out-param as `DWORD dwGot` (with the why inline) — all other WinAPI out-params in the TU were already correct (`DWORD dwRead` header read, `DWORD dwWritten` atomic write); the two correct sites masked the one bad pattern in review, which is exactly what the new tripwire now pins. No binary-format change, no behavior change (both types are 32-bit unsigned on win32 — only the C++ name identity differed). **Gate**: new row `stash: sss io DWORD outparam` pins `DWORD dwGot` in VanillaPlusStash.cpp (suite **281/281**); self-id row + Application.cpp re-pinned R64; SMOKE build line + item 0 + §H header now say R64; plan status + FEATURES row note the fix round. **Field-verify ask unchanged**: SMOKE §H items 21-27 on the R64 build (`d2boot.log` line 1 must say `Vanilla+0.0.6-R64`). **Delivery**: overlay `vanilla-plus-0.0.6-R64-stash1p-fix.zip` (the full 14-file set at R64 — applies over a fresh 1783-synced tree OR directly over an already-applied R63 overlay, idempotent) + git bundle `vanilla-plus-1776-1783-R64-stash1p-fix.bundle` (strict fast-forward of the R63 bundle).*)

Then 100."** — which resolves all four plan §9 calls at once (1 page, retail grid; stash-panel tab, **no** hotkey; **shared gold in v1** — supersedes the plan's personal-only recommendation; file `_d2vp_shared.sss`), and merges plan rounds R63+R64 into one shippable increment; the hardening round (failure drills + the retail `.d2s` round-trip) stays as the follow-up. **Architecture (zero new packets, zero new item bytes, zero server container code):** while the shared page is active, the player's retail page-4 stash inventory HOLDS the shared set — real item units materialized from the `.sss` file by the retail save-stream loader (`PLRSAVE_DispatchItemLoadByFormat`, format 0x60, the same call the trade-cancel restore uses; placement comes from each record's own location bits) — so every retail stash flow (grid draw, 0x19 pick, 0x18 place, 0x1F swap) works unchanged; the personal set waits in a fork-owned `'JM'` memory snapshot written by the retail per-item encoder (`Items::SerializeItem`, the `.d2s` item-block bytes). Toggle = snapshot → dematerialize (`ITEM_RemoveItemFromCurrentLocation`, which also pushes the client 0x9D removes) → materialize the other set → push 0x9C puts for everything that landed. **Shared gold borrows the retail `UNITSTAT_goldbank` stat while the page is active**: the retail deposit (0x4F/0x14) / withdraw (0x4F/0x13) buttons and the stash gold display keep working verbatim but move shared gold; the toggle swaps the borrow (personal snapshot ↔ shared pool) and the save flush absorbs whatever the retail buttons moved. **I1 by construction**: `VPSHARED_FlushBeforeSave` sits at the top of `PlayerSave::SaveGameAllGameTypes` (the single dispatcher every save path goes through) — before ANY `.d2s` is built, the shared set is parked into `<save dir>\_d2vp_shared.sss` (own 20-byte header: magic `VPS1`, version 1, pageCount 1 — the 10/100-page future is a version-2 header extension — gold u32, blockSize, additive checksum; atomic write temp→`MoveFileExA` replace, one-generation `.bak`; six degrade marks, file never touched by a misread) and the personal set + personal goldbank come back. **Client**: a "Shared" text tab top-right on the stash panel (font-drawn, same row as the stash gold label; white = personal, green `> Shared` = shared; **no hotkey** — tab-only by spec), hit-tested in `INV_HandleStashLeftClick`, drawn after the gold-button draw; the click sends the C->S 0x4F Click-Button packet with the out-of-retail-range button id **0x7D** + desired page in the param word (a retail server would reject it harmlessly — scope guard: the server handles it only when `[features] shared_stash=1` AND `SGAMETYPE_SINGLEPLAYER`; other game types inert). Per-game client reset rides the wholesale UI reset beside `ALTTOGGLE_Reset`. **Files**: NEW `D2Game/PLAYER/VanillaPlusStash.{h,cpp}` (fork-owned server TU: state, `.sss` I/O, toggles, flush); thin VP sites in `D2Game/PLAYER/PlayerTrade.cpp` (ClickButton intercept), `D2Game/PLAYER/PlayerSave.cpp` (save flush), `D2Client/UI/Inv.cpp` (tab draw + click), `D2Client/UI/Draw.cpp` (per-game reset); `D2Client/UI/VanillaPlusCore.{h,cpp}` (gate + tab + ini template row `shared_stash=0` documented); self-ID + gate re-pin. **Gates**: 25 new `stash:` rows (sites, includes, ini keys, magic, marks, atomic write, .bak, goldbank borrow, retail encoder/loader/remove/0x9C, SP guard) → suite **280/280**. **Field-verify ask**: SMOKE **§H** (new, items 21-27) — tab presence/gate-off, the char-A-deposits/char-B-withdraws round trip, gold deposit+withdraw with personal gold intact, the `.d2s` loads in retail after a shared session (I1), per-game reset, and the one-minute checksum degrade drill. **Delivery**: overlay `vanilla-plus-0.0.6-R63-shared-stash-1p.zip` (all 14 changed files: the new VanillaPlusStash TU pair, the 4 VP-site files (PlayerTrade / PlayerSave / Inv / Draw), the VanillaPlusCore pair, Application.cpp self-ID, the gates, and the 4 docs (handoff / SMOKE §H / plan status / FEATURES row); apply over the 1783-synced tree) + git bundle `vanilla-plus-1776-1783-R63-stash1p.bundle` (basis origin/main `9b97cea`; `git fetch <bundle> main:main` fast-forwards).*)

**game entry 2's loader reads count 3, walks three sub-paths, hits the empty third path → `HaltTail(0x501)` → process death. Theory ranked 1-2-3 in `ISSUE-19-BODY.md` (paste-ready): 1 = corruptor second-session landing, 2 = the merc re-creation cluster (the one path R54's no-merc verification never exercised — `MERC_HireAndReplaceMerc` returns on `RollMercStats` FALSE AFTER the merc unit is flagged/node-created and BEFORE `RecastPetAndAssign`, leaving a petdata-less merc whose `SaveMercData` read `pPetData->nType` unguarded = guaranteed NULL deref on the next save), 3 = stale cross-session client state (`Roster::pRosterPartyList` was the only roster-adjacent global `ClearBothRosterLists` never cleaned). **Shipped R60 (probes + guards, zero behavior change on healthy state)**: `D2Client/GAME/Roster.cpp` — `ClearBothRosterLists` logs `roster: clear lists players=%p other=%p partyList=%p [R60]` then frees the party list (idempotent; closes candidate 3); `D2Client/GAME/SCmd.cpp` — the 0x5B handler logs `roster state: players=%p other=%p partyList=%p [R60]` right after the existing playerjoin mark (join-time state, pre-regroup); `D2Game/Monster/MonsterAI.cpp` — `merc: RollMercStats FALSE - petdata not registered [R60]` on the early-out (names candidate 2's site the moment it fires); `D2Game/PLAYER/PlayerSave2.cpp` — `SaveMercData` null-guards the petdata (`save: merc petdata missing - merc fields skipped [R60]`; the merc is omitted from that .d2s instead of crashing the save path) + `SaveCorpseItems` initializes the corpse-flags dword to 1 (was uninitialized stack garbage written verbatim into every corpse record; real 1.10+ files carry 01 00 00 00 — determinism, loader-side inert). **Gates**: 6 new rows (exit probe, party-list free, 0x5B probe, merc guard, corpse init, roll-fail mark), suite **240 → 246/246**. R84 the item-tooltip zero fix (issue #16, owner: items dropping with "0 poison damage over 0 seconds" and 'of Frost' items with "+0 cold damage"): root cause display-side in `SKILLDESC_BuildStatDescription` (`D2Client/UI/SkillDesc.cpp`, upstream-faithful, zero prior fork edits) — the single-value branches (fire 0xe1c, lightning 0xe20, magic 0xe22, cold 0xe1e, poison 0xe24) called `UNICODE_FormatWideString` with NO varargs, so any roll whose range collapses to min==max printed the stack residue (zeros in our builds) instead of the number; the range siblings in the same switch DO pass (min,max[,seconds]), and Ghidra is consistent within one function, so the missing args are what the retail 1.14d binary does — a latent retail bug the fork's build layout makes visible; trigger data-verified against the 1.13/1.14d tables: 'of Frost' (MagicSuffix, weap) rolls `cold-max 1..2` with `cold-min` FIXED 1 (cold-len 50), so every drop rolling a 1 collapsed (fire/ltng/magic suffixes can't roll min==max — max rolls 2..5/6..8/6..11 — which is why only cold/poison ever showed it); poison collapses through the `len_disp = poisonlength/poison_count` rounding in the display formula `(raw*len_disp+0x80)>>8` (and any length-0 poison content lands in the same branch — the client's poisonlength only ever arrives via the item bitstream deserializer, Items.cpp:5191, and the serializer writes whatever the server statlist holds, zero included). Fix (5 VP-marked sites, one per branch): pass the same values the sibling range branches pass — elemental (min,max), poison (value,seconds) — extra varargs harmless in cdecl; item stats were never wrong, tooltip-only, no generation change. Gates 308 → 311 (`itemdesc: zero-fix VP marks` x5, `elemental single-value varargs` x8, `poison single-value varargs` x2; self-id row re-pinned R84), self-ID `Vanilla+0.0.7-R83` → `Vanilla+0.0.7-R84`; fork TUs 4/4 host-compiled (SkillDesc.cpp sits outside the 4-TU syntax net — the new call shapes verified via a host g++ varargs stub, the R83 EscMenu pattern); SMOKE-CHECKLIST.md gains §J (items 37-38: cold tooltip never "+0", poison tooltip never "0 over 0" — screenshot the item if a zero/zero ever still appears: that would be genuine length-0 content, a generation-side follow-up); template regenerated (41 open boxes). Field ask = §J items 37-38 on the R84 build; Rev 109 this record; docs line current through Rev 109)

*Rev 104 (2026-11-02, code round — **the waypoint row-icon art fixed at 720p (issue #21): the two `waygateicons` draws were the only site in the panel that never rode the wide-Y term** — R80 → **R81**, self-ID bumped `Vanilla+0.0.7-R80` → `Vanilla+0.0.7-R81`, gate count unchanged 299): owner filed #21 ("[720p] Art not showing on Waypoints Panel", narrowed in chat: the per-waypoint button icons). Root cause, all code-pinned: in `DRAW_UI_Waypoint` every top-anchored 800-space Y carries `+ VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()` from the site-6 retrofit (`cf2c9e59 "720p site-6"`) — the act tabs (:477/:513), the open/close button (:524), the row texts (:556), the cancel tooltip (:569/:570), the header line (:580) — and the input path inversely virtualizes mouse Y (`nMouseY + gnPanelOffsetY - VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()`, :453/:673/:741), so clicks land on the moved rows. The two `WaypointWaygateIcons` row draws (:541 normal-state, :553 current-area blue) kept the vanilla decompile shape `pDrawOffsets[1] - gnPanelOffsetY` — byte-identical since the initial recovery (`4b0154cd`): the retrofit missed them, and the 1794/1801/1806 regen waves carried the vanilla-shaped lines through untouched (upstream never reshapes fork marker lines, so the merge audits had nothing to flag). Fix: `+ VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()` + the `// VP: [resolution site-6]` marker on both draws — at 800x600 the offset is 0, so retail stays byte-identical; only modes 3/4 move. ### §9.1 Track C record as it stood at rev 109 (verbatim)

### 9.1 Track C — minion skeletons gold — **ROOT-CAUGHT v2 + FIXED R51, field-verify pending (issue #8)** — the uniques half is CLOSED (R49, issue #9, record in H26)

- **R51 root cause (skeleton symptom, this round — the R50 field pass FALSIFIED the roster-only theory)**: owner on the R50 build: **"Necro Skeletons are still yellow."** The second write site — `CreateObject`'s MonStats2 `Utrans==0xFF` ownership fallback (`D2Client/UNIT/CUnit.cpp`, packet path via `SCmd.cpp:523`) — wrote `(uint32_t)!uMercType`: the same inverted polarity, at spawn time, and the event-driven roster recolors (roster-update packet handler only) never fire for SP summons — so the spawn idx 1 (yellow hostile table) stuck for the unit's whole life. Rev 55's documented off-by-4 read-base fallback family EXONERATED: a `+4` shift rotates each 256-byte palette by 4 entries, it cannot swap the 0/1 tables. Baseline transcription defect (verbatim at `44e3518`; last inverted write in the tree). Fix: raw flag at spawn + [R51] comment; both sites agree on the raw-flag convention; gate `spawn: ownership palshift polarity (R51)`, suite 205/205. Full record: Rev 56.
- **R50 root cause (skeleton symptom)**: the roster pet-recolor wrote `(uint32_t)!bHostile` into both `SetGfxSequenceData` (= dwCofDataFlags, the re-sync latch shadow) and `SetPalShiftIndex` — inverting the palshift.dat sub-table pair (own/ally pet must read sub-table 0 = bone-white via raw bHostile=0; hostile owner gets sub-table 1 = yellow tell). Every own raised skeleton read the yellow HOSTILE table. Baseline transcription defect (identical `44e3518` → main; all upstream branches carry it). Fix: 4 writes flipped to raw `bHostile` + [R50] comments; gate `roster: pet recolor polarity (R50)`, suite 204/204. Full record: Rev 55. **Field disposition: necessary but not sufficient — the roster site is event-driven and inert for SP summons; the spawn site (R51 above) is the one that decides the first render.**
- **Next discriminator (if R51 still field-fails)**: the remaining suspect family is the palshift.dat entry parse itself (loader table order vs retail) — force idx 2 (TransLvl path) on a skeleton and compare its render against the idx 0/1 renders. The mechanism map (where retail colors live: static palettes / RandTransforms roll / draw-time application / seed inheritance), the closed uniques record (R49 root cause + field verification + the retired issue #9 proposal + the not-a-dropped-fix audit) and the track-methodology bullets moved verbatim to **H26 §9.1 closed half** at rev 58.

### 9.7 Track M — save a game, re-enter crashes — **ROOT-CAUGHT + FIXED R61, FIELD-VERIFIED R61 — CLOSED ([issue #19](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/19))**

- Owner symptom (verbatim): "Saving a game and then trying to enter a game again crashes the game" — a SECOND-session defect (first entry after boot reportedly fine).
- Audit of record (R60): regression window clean (R54→R59 touches no save/join TU); `.d2s` save/load engine byte-symmetric across all ten blocks (checksum/size validation identical both sides; client readers exactly 0x2000-bounded); the party regroup correct on clean data — the 2026-10-31 R54 `roster heal:` cap marks therefore = genuine corruption (corruptor alive, standing watch).
- Theory ranking: (1) corruptor second-session landing (heap reuse); (2) merc re-creation cluster — `RollMercStats` FALSE leaves a flagged, node-created merc without its pet-data node (pre-R60: unguarded `pPetData->nType` in `SaveMercData` = NULL deref on the next save); (3) stale `Roster::pRosterPartyList` across sessions (the only roster global without exit cleanup — now freed).
- R60 shipped: exit + join roster-state probes (`roster: clear lists …` / `roster state: …`), the petdata guard + roll-fail mark, corpse-flags determinism (01 00 00 00). Suite 246/246, self-ID `Vanilla+0.0.6-R60`, SMOKE §G items 18-20, overlay `vanilla-plus-0.0.6-R60-issue-19-instrumentation.zip`.
- Field log 2026-10-31 (R60 build) READ: exit probe all-NULL (candidate 3 dead), join probe all-NULL, two `roster heal:` cap marks on session 1's load (corruptor watch), session 2 `HALT line=1281 (0x501)` 47 ms after the first server tick - symbolized via the owner's d2exe.map to ESCMENU_LoadLocalizedImages_HaltTail (empty sub-image path) <- ESCMENU_Initialize <- InitializeUI. ROOT CAUSE = the VP Resolution row's runtime table mutation (see Rev 72); merc candidate (#2) never fired; stale-roster candidate (#3) closed by the probes. R61 = the fix.
- Next discriminator: the ONE field run per SMOKE §G items 18-19 (two game entries in one process; the fix is proven by NOT crashing + the one escmenu mark + the Resolution row intact).
- Owner verdict (2026-11-01, R61 build): **"R61 Confirmed fixes #19"** — the §G fix-proof run passed: the second entry loaded and played; no `halt report:`, no `d2crash-*.txt`. Track M CLOSED; paste-ready close record = `ISSUE-19-BODY.md` (status header FIELD-CONFIRMED). HISTORY.md archive ritual on owner call (the track I/J pattern). The R60 probes stay shipped; the roster-heal corruptor watch stays ARMED (§6).

  ([#19](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/19)); the **shared stash**

  compile-fix rounds — the merge-site syntax sweep, `scripts/merge_site_syntax_sweep.sh`, now guards the merge surface); the first post-1792 build

  - **Necro minion skeleton coloring**
    ([#8](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/8)). The spawn-time palette
    write carried an inverted hostility flag, and the roster recolor (event-driven,
    roster-update packets only) never rewrites SP summons — every own raised skeleton
    spawned straight into the yellow hostile palette. Re-fixed in the R51 round (the raw
    hostility flag is written at spawn too); field verification pending.

Right-aligned via the rendered advance width (`DRAW_text0`), draw y moved to row bottom + 2; geometry is resolution-independent, so 800×600 picks up the same corrected placement. Two new gates pin the anchor. Record: [`handoff.md`](handoff.md) round journal. |
| R84 (pending tag) | The item-tooltip zero fix ([#16](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/16)): items could drop with a mod line reading **"0 poison damage over 0 seconds"**, and 'of Frost' gear could show **"+0 Cold Damage"**. Display-side root cause: the item-stat tooltip formatter's single-value branches (fire/lightning/magic/cold/poison) called the wide-string formatter **without the numeric varargs**, so any roll whose range collapses to min==max printed stack residue instead of the value ('of Frost' weapons roll `cold-max` 1..2 with `cold-min` fixed at 1 — every drop that rolls a 1 collapsed; poison collapses through the length rounding). The fix passes the same values the range branches pass; item stats were never wrong — tooltip-only. Three new gates. Record: [`handoff.md`](handoff.md) round journal. |

## H35. Track P — the A1Q2 Bloodraven turn-in arc (issue #22) — ROOT-CAUGHT + FIXED R85, field-verify pending (rev 114, 2026-11-02) + trim #11

- **Issue**: owner, 2026-11-02 ([issue #22](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/22)): "**Bug** A1Q2 Bloodraven killed unable to turn in". Track P opened at rev 113 (the five-open-issue triage; #22/#18 had never been triaged) as the §6 gossip-data watch HIT — A1Q2 is on the H28 family-audit list of singular-table quests.
- **Root cause (rev 114, static — the track-J truncation family, H28)**: the regen emitted `A1Q2::aNpcGossipData` SINGULAR (1 slot = the accept entry `{Kashya 0x96, str 0x51, menu 1}`), identical at baseline `44e3518`. `Q02_Callback00_NpcActivate` picks **slot 3** whenever QUESTSTATE_RewardAwaits is set ("return for reward"); `AddGossipMessageOnMonster` reads `pNpcMsgTable[3]` with no bounds check → +0x24C past the object → Kashya never delivered a Blood Raven turn-in line → the client never echoed `c2s 0x31` with msg 0x5c → `Q02_Callback11_ScrollMessage`'s reward gate (0x5c → `HireMercFromNpc(0x96)`, the rogue mercenary) was never asked. Same silent shape as issue #13: accept (slot 0, in-bounds) always worked, the reward step (slot 3, OOB) died. The R54 generic chain marks (gossip add / 0x27 / overhead / npc menu / c2s 0x31) make the site visible in one field log with no new shared-code instrumentation.
- **Fix (R85, rev 114; self-ID → `Vanilla+0.0.7-R85`)**: `globals/D2Game.cpp` — `A1Q2::aNpcGossipData` → **5-slot array** (VP `[gossip-table-fix]`): slot 0 = accept preserved (`{0x96, 0x51, menu 1}`); slot 3 = turn-in `{0x96, 0x5c, menu 0}` — pinned by `Q02_Callback11_ScrollMessage`'s own gate (0x51 accept / 0x5c reward; bMenu 0 mirrors the proven R54 A1Q1 entry, bMenu 1 converts to 0 at add time anyway); flavor slots 1/2/4 (state 2/3 progress + participant lines) left EMPTY — string ids not in the tree, not invented. R54 build-repair lesson pre-applied: extern `globals.h` re-declared `[5]` (a widened definition makes the singular extern a conflicting declaration), and `pNpcMessages = &A1Q2::aNpcGossipData` became a plain array-decay assignment. **7 Kashya chain marks** in A1Q2.cpp (mirror the R53 A1Q1 set) + the `support/d2_boot.h` include: activate dump (rewardAwaits/completed/stateFlag), the three gossip-pick marks (line 3 reward / participant line 4 / state-lookup line), `kashya gossip msg=` echo, `turn-in gate REJECTED`, `bloodraven reward GRANTED (rogue merc)`. Gates: 9 new rows + the self-ID row repinned R84→R85, suite **311 → 320/320**. Sandbox g++ harness `scripts/rev114_a1q2_gossip_harness.py`: compiles the SHIPPED initializer against the real packed structs — 0xC4 table, all five slot values, the +0x24C OOB demonstration, and the slot-3 walk adding str=0x005c menu=0 (accept walk intact).
- **Field expectation (one log, the discriminator chain)**: `quest: kashya activate classId=0x0096 rewardAwaits=1 completed=0 ...` → `quest: kashya activate -> gossip line 3 (reward)` → `quest: gossip add quest=0x02 slot=3 npc=0x0096` + `str=0x005c menu=0 -> added` → `quest: npc interact 0x27 send` → `s2c 0x27 overhead msgType0=...0005c` (raw dword print; menu-decoded `0x005c`) → `npc menu: msgType0=0x005c` → `c2s 0x31 gossip send gossip=0x005c` → `quest: kashya gossip msg=0x005c` → **`quest: bloodraven reward GRANTED (rogue merc)`** (merc arrives; post-grant activate reads `rewardAwaits=0 completed=1`, participant line 4 → slot 4 EMPTY → generic menu, the designed fallback). If the log instead shows `gossip add slot=3 EMPTY` / `NO CLASS MATCH`, the fix did not reach the build — check the d2boot.log line-1 self-ID first.
- **Trim #11 (rev 114)**: the rev-113 round record (below, verbatim) moved here from handoff.md to hold the standing 16 kb handoff budget; docs line current through Rev 114.

*Verbatim from handoff.md, moved at rev 114 (2026-11-02) — trim #11: the rev-113 record (five-open-issue triage). Round content is unchanged and live: the GitHub issue sweep (owner: "there are 5 open issues, not 1"), tracks S/R/P/Q opened, §9 recompressed, §9.5–9.7 stubs retired, trim #10 → H34.*

## H37. The -nointro boot switch (R86, rev 116, 2026-11-02) + trim #13

- **Feature (owner ask: "Can we add a -nointro command line parameter to skip the two intro videos")**: `d2vp.exe -nointro`. The two logo .biks (`New_BLIZ640x480/240.bik` + `BlizNorth640x480/240.bik`) play on EVERY launch inside `PlaysBlizzardNorthCutScene` (MainMenus.cpp:4357 boot path, "Show intro and first screen without menus"), and the title screen auto-fires the `d2intro` .bik from its timer form (`gaPredefinedForms[1]` -> `UIMENU_PlayIntroAndReturnToMainScreen`, the same handler the click uses). First-run also auto-plays the D2 (`d2video.mpq` no-bnet-reg path) or LoD (`D2x_Intro` + the Aux Battle.net 0x80 played-once bit) cinematic inside the same two functions.
- **Design (retail-path reuse, minimal surface)**: a fork-owned one-shot parse `VPNOINTRO_IsRequested()` in `VanillaPlusCore.{h,cpp}` (R86) reads `GetCommandLineA()` once — whitespace-separated token exactly `-nointro`, case-insensitive, one wrapping quote pair stripped (Windows launchers quote args), 32-byte token cap so long tokens can never match — and marks d2boot.log once (`[vp] cmdline -nointro: boot intro videos disabled`). The retail `D2IniConfigValues[58]` table, `D2IniConfigStrc` (packed retail layout) and the Fog parser are NOT touched. The two boot/title cutscene entries gate their `BeginCutSceneAtResolutionMode0()` call on the flag: with the switch, `bCutSceneStarted` reads 0 and each site's retail begin-failed fallthrough (no cutscene, straight to the next screen — the exact state path retail takes when BeginCutScene itself fails) becomes the boot path. `SetResolutionModeWithEndCutScene(1)` still runs (retail does it unconditionally). In-game quest cutscenes ride a different call path and stay vanilla; without the switch the flag reads 0 — boot byte-for-byte vanilla.
- **Sites**: `D2Launch/MainMenus.cpp` — the include + two identical VP-marked ternaries (`UIMENU_PlayIntroAndReturnToMainScreen` + `PlaysBlizzardNorthCutScene`); `D2Client/UI/VanillaPlusCore.{h,cpp}` — the parse + mark; `D2Client/Engine/Application.cpp:66` — self-ID `Vanilla+0.0.7-R85` -> `Vanilla+0.0.7-R86`.
