### Registry watch (owner environment, 2026-11-03 — not a build bug)

One D3D session "didn't go in game" across many builds **including R83** (owner smoke-tested) until the registry was restored to fresh 1.14d install values — a corrupted D2 video-options registry value (owner suspicion: one of the slider values). Vanilla+ itself writes no registry values (d2vp.ini lives next to the exe; `portable_save=1` explicitly avoids the registry). If D3D ever again boots to a black screen / fails to enter game across builds that previously worked: restore the fresh-install video-settings registry values before suspecting the build, and note which value was corrupt.

Glide can toggle." — with `no_panel_shift=0` the R95 code is inert at the toggle moment (options menu = border 0/3, every gate call site retail), so the feature itself is not the demonstrated trigger. R96 ships five **one-shot** `d2boot.log` probes (zero behavior change) and asks for ONE repro on the R96 build, in D3D, at 1280x720:

- [x] **46. Toggle repro with artifacts:** in D3D, open VIDEO OPTIONS (this writes `videoopt: perspective load reg=<v> capable=<c>`), toggle Perspective once. After the death, send (a) the newest `d2crash-*.txt` next to the exe — or state plainly that **none** was written, (b) the last ~30 lines of `d2boot.log`, (c) whether the death is instant at the slider release or only after closing the menu, (d) whether both directions die (OFF→ON and ON→OFF). — **ANSWERED 2026-11-03 (R96 field pass, D3D 800x600):** `videoopt: perspective load reg=1 capable=1 [R96]` at load, ~6.5 s of perspective-ON play (all one-shot marks fired cleanly), then `videoopt: perspective apply renderer=6 active=1 -> 0 [R96]` and `halt report: line=200 addr=00F0C70C` **in the same 15 ms tick** — Redivivus: `HALT line=200 at d2vp.exe+0x21C70C`, Fog unrecoverable-error halt, no access violation. Line=200 = the D3D floor/subtile texture-cache **promote range checks** (`D3DFLOOR_PromoteNodeToMRU` / `D3DSUBTILE_PromoteNodeToMRU`, `nIndex > nMaxNodes`); the death is on the first world frame with perspective OFF, the frame that first runs the perspective-OFF wall/shadow builders. Root-cause audit in CHANGELOG [R97]; R97 ships the guards below.

## Evidence to keep if anything breaks

| Artifact | Comes from | Tells us |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `d2boot.log` | `d2vp.exe` | build self-ID (which round you actually ran) |
| `d2crash-<date>-<time>.txt` | `d2vp.exe` (auto-written next to the exe; hangwatch dumps one after 12 s) | symbolised crashed module/function/offset |
| `glide3x.log` | glide3xvk wrapper | frame, preset, chain state; whether the faulting thread was wrapper code |

A crash without both artifacts cannot be attributed — attach all to the issue.

## H44. Issue #32 — the Gheed gamble click-crash: root cause + fix R128 (rev 136, 2026-11-06)

**Owner report** ([issue #32](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2VP-QoL/issues/32)): "Clicking 'Gamble' at Gheed crashes the game." Field context, second message: "This isn't a recent regression, I tested R108, R98, even R67. All crash upon clicking Gamble." — i.e. the crash is coeval with the source regeneration itself, which put it in the **baseline-regen-defect class** (the R54 A1Q1 gossip-table truncation's twin) before the investigation even started.

**The crash shape** (VEH log pasted in-session, R127 build): NPC menu opens through the no-echo branch — `npc menu: generic menu (no 0x31 echo)` (`ui.cpp` quest-gossip-mark, the designed fallback when the 0x27 batch carries no scroll msg, `msgType0=0xffff` — Gheed has no quest gossip, this branch is *correct* for him) → ~1.08 s later (the human click on the Gamble button) → `VEH: fatal exception 0xC0000005 at addr 00F0DE47`, `own-code fault, 23 frames collected`, `AV READ at 31A37000`, report `d2crash-20261106-005333.txt`. Image base 0x00B90000 → eip offset **+0x7DE47**. The 23-frame stack stayed in the owner's report file (only eip was pasted) — symbolization turned out to be unnecessary: the log shape alone pinned the flow.

**What runs on the click**: client `NPCMENU_RequestGamble` → c2s NPC action → server `SUNITNPC_ProcessNpcAction(nMode≠0)` → interaction registered `bGambling=1` → `SUNITNPC_ValidateNpcInteraction` → `GAMBLE_GenerateGambleInventory` (SUNITNPC.cpp). SP latency ≈ one server tick — fits the ~1 s post-click fault.

**Root cause (static, proven on the data)**: the vanilla gamble roll reads the **per-level upper-bound cache** (`gnItemTblsCacheSize[100]`, built by `TXT_AllocTxt_gamble`: `[0]=2`, `[1..99]` = count of gamble.txt items with item-level ≤ that level) through a **Ghidra data-adjacency artifact** — `nItemTableIdx = (&pGambleTable[1].nSize)[level]`, i.e. byte address `nTxtGambleSize + 8 + 4*level` (`D2GambleTableStrc` = `{int nSize; void* pTable}`, so `[1].nSize` = base+8; level clamped 5..99 → reads base+0x1C..base+0x194). In the **original binary** the 100-int cache sat immediately after the 8-byte gamble struct in `.data`, so the arithmetic was exactly `cache[level]`. The regen expressed the *code* faithfully but re-homed the *data*: `nTxtGambleSize` stayed a file-scope static (ItemTbls.cpp:69, 8 bytes) while `gnItemTblsCacheSize` became a **function-local static** inside `TXT_AllocTxt_gamble` — the adjacency is unreproducible across separate storage objects, so every read lands in **unrelated .bss neighbors**: `nTxtItemRatioSize`, `pTxtItemRatio` (a **heap pointer** once itemratio.txt loads), `nTxtQualityItemsSize`, `nTxtLowQualityItemsSize`, `gsBooksTable`, `pTxtBooks`… Consequences by garbage value: ≤ 0 → bound clamped 0 → every roll = `pTable[0]` (silent wrongness); pointer-sized value → `idx = seedLow % ptr` → `uRandLow = *(pTable + idx*4)` — a wild read tens/hundreds of MB past the table → **the 0x31A37000 AV READ**. Meanwhile the loader's carefully built cache was **dead data, read by no one** — the repo-wide grep for the adjacency pattern found exactly one consumer (SUNITNPC.cpp:2124; the `SpriteCache.cpp` `[1].nSize` hit is a real struct-array member, unrelated).

**Fix (R128)**, three files + self-ID:
- `D2Common/DATATBLS/ItemTbls.cpp` — `gnItemTblsCacheSize[100]` moved to **file scope next to `nTxtGambleSize`** (it was one of the original globals; guards kept); accessor **`GetGambleLevelCache(int32_t)`** added after `GetGambleTableSize` — bounds-checked, returns 0 outside [0,99] so a caller bug degrades to an empty roll, never a wild index.
- `D2Common/DATATBLS/ItemTbls.h` — the accessor declared beside `GetGambleTableSize`.
- `D2Game/UNIT/SUNITNPC.cpp` — the adjacency artifact replaced by `GetGambleLevelCache((int)pPlayerUnit)` (the `pPlayerUnit` slot holds the clamped 5..99 roll level at that point); `[gamble-cache-fix R128]` VP markers both sides; `support/d2_boot.h` included.
- `Game/Main.cpp:1078` — self-ID `Vanilla+0.0.8-R127` → **`Vanilla+0.0.8-R128`**.

**Field marks (d2boot.log, the §M discriminators)**: `gamble: gen start npc=0x%04x level=%d cache=%d total=%d` (cache must read a sane count, not a pointer dword) → ~14 × `gamble: roll level=%d bound=%u pick=%u` (bound = the cache value, 2..total; pick = the rolled item class id) → `gamble: gen done items=%d`. A garbage-looking `bound=` on a future log means the exe predates the fix.

**Family audit**: `SUNITNPC_ValidateGambleItem` / `SUNITNPC_ProcessGambleResult` (merc/pet paths) and `SUNITNPC_ProcessGamble` (regen-named, actually the repair handler — smith classId-guarded) are clean; no other `[1].nSize`-style adjacency consumers exist. The gamble **purchase** path becomes first-time reachable with this fix — SMOKE §M3 exercises it.

**Gates**: 9 new rows (self-id R128 + R127-gone + cache file-scope + accessor def/decl + consumer-uses-accessor + adjacency-artifact-absent(count 0) + the three `gamble:` mark pins) → suite **510 → 519/519**. SMOKE-CHECKLIST §M added (4 items); CHANGELOG human-lane entry; handoff rev 136. **No sandbox build** (owner directive: owner/CI build — sandbox builds time out and reset; the owner's MSYS2 build stays the compile gate of record, handoff §8). Overlay: `d2vp-r128-gamble-fix-overlay.zip`.

**Field verify (rev 137, 2026-11-06): PASS — owner closed [#32](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2VP-QoL/issues/32).** The §M items went green on the R128 build: the gamble window opens with a mixed item list and no crash, the `gamble:` chain reads sane (`gen start` cache/total counts, `roll` bounds 2..total — never a pointer-looking dword, `gen done items=14`), one purchase landed in inventory with gold deducted, and a same-session re-open produced a fresh list. The purchase path — first-time reachable with this fix — exercised clean. Track T closes as a **baseline-regen defect, field-proven fixed** (the R54 gossip class's twin); SMOKE §M stays as the standing gamble regression check. **Shipped as tag [`R128`](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2VP-QoL/releases/tag/R128) the same day** — the 0.0.8 release notes posted verbatim; CHANGELOG promoted to [0.0.8] at handoff rev 138.

Both reports predate the R128 tag — pin the builds via `d2boot.log` line 1 before triage. Self-ID stays **`Vanilla+0.0.8-R128`** (docs-only round; next code round = **R129**), gates **519/519**. Build state: release line **0.0.8**, rounds tracked as `-Rxx`; upstream synced to Ghidra **1948** (eight numbered syncs — details H39/H40 + the R105/R123 entries in CHANGELOG/H41). NEWEST code round = **R128, the gamble-cache fix (issue #32 — owner: clicking Gamble at Gheed crashes; tested back to R67, all crash)** — NOT a regression, a baseline regen defect, the data-adjacency twin of the R54 gossip-table class: the gamble roll in `D2Game/UNIT/SUNITNPC.cpp` `GAMBLE_GenerateGambleInventory` read the per-level upper-bound cache via vanilla .data adjacency — `(&pGambleTable[1].nSize)[level]` = `nTxtGambleSize+8+4*level`, valid only where the 100-int cache sat adjacent in the original binary; the regen had moved the cache into a `TXT_AllocTxt_gamble` function-local static, so the read hit unrelated .bss neighbors (`nTxtItemRatioSize`, the `pTxtItemRatio` heap pointer, the quality/books table structs) — a pointer read as a roll bound → `seed % ptr` → `pGambleTable->pTable + idx*4` = the user's own-code AV READ 0x31A37000 (eip image +0x7DE47), ~1 s after the click, on every rev since the regen; the loader's carefully built cache was dead data, read by no one. Fix: cache moved to file scope next to `nTxtGambleSize` (`ItemTbls.cpp`), bounds-checked accessor `GetGambleLevelCache` (`ItemTbls.h`/`.cpp`, 0 outside [0,99]), consumer reads the accessor (`SUNITNPC.cpp`); the adjacency artifact is gate-pinned ABSENT (count 0); `gamble:` field marks added (gen start / roll / gen done — SMOKE §M2 discriminators: `bound=` must be a sane count 2..total, never a pointer-looking dword). The crash report carries 23 frames but only eip was pasted — the log shape + adjacency math made symbolization unnecessary. **The §M field pass went green 2026-11-06 and the owner closed the issue** (record H44 carries the verify addendum). **No sandbox build this round** (owner directive 2026-11-06: owner/CI build — sandbox builds time out and reset); compile gate = the owner's MSYS2 build; sandbox side ran the grep gate suite only.
